# Smoke20 Comparative Experiment Report

- **Experiment ID:** `kdc-smoke20-all-registered-final-20261012T074200`
- **Pipeline status:** `PARTIAL`
- **Git commit:** `f28b4e3866a51575ea47dddb3362694d84c6de25` (dirty=True)
- **Dataset profile:** `smoke20`; selected instances=20
- **Objectives:** minmax, minsum, minmaxsum
- **Selected exact reference backend:** `branch-and-bound`
- **Reproduction command:** `bash scripts/run_experiment.sh --pipeline smoke --dataset data/instances/public_instance_set --algorithms all-registered --modes all --exact-reference auto --threads 2 --with-animations --animation-policy all-solutions --build-dir build/prompt8-clean-20261012 --output results/experiments/kdc-smoke20-all-registered-final-20261012T074200 --resume`
- **Run slots:** planned=660, terminal=660, status counts=`{"COMPLETED": 487, "FAILED": 51, "INVALID": 39, "SKIPPED": 60, "TIME_LIMIT": 23}`

## Scientific interpretation

This is a small smoke dataset, not a scaling study. With one repeat, reported values are a single-run smoke comparison. No significance tests, confidence claims, universal speedups, or general algorithm-superiority conclusions are made.
Runtime quantiles include only completed runs with an explicit `solve_time_sec`; time-limited and timed-out runs are counted separately as censored outcomes. Wall, solve, verification, serialization and total times remain separate. Missing measurements remain null.
MinMaxSum is the two-component vector `(peak_cost, integral_cost)`; no scalar score is manufactured. Empirical ratios to exact are included only against the one designated valid, proven, continuously verified exact reference and are not theoretical approximation ratios.
EMPIRICAL/sample verification is reported separately from continuous verification. A rendered solution is not by itself a feasibility, verification, or optimality proof.
